"""
EasyChatBot Application Bootstrap.

Responsible for application startup composition.

Responsibilities:

- create application-level services;
- compose approved Runtime dependencies;
- register configured services.

This module does not:

- execute agents;
- execute capabilities;
- contain business logic;
- expose runtime state;
- serve API requests.

Runtime construction remains delegated to:

    src.ai_runtime.composition
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from backend.application.factory import (
    ApplicationFactory,
)

from backend.runtime.external_runtime_container import (
    configure_external_runtime_service,
    get_external_runtime_service as _get_external_runtime_service,
)

from backend.services.external_runtime_service import (
    ExternalRuntimeService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.composition import (
    create_external_runtime_gateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.parallel import (
    ParallelPodcastProvider,
)

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    IntelligenceProcessor,
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


_bootstrapped = False


def bootstrap_application() -> None:
    """
    Bootstrap application dependencies.

    Creates the ExternalRuntimeService and registers it
    inside the application runtime container.
    """

    global _bootstrapped

    if _bootstrapped:
        return

    factory = ApplicationFactory()

    gateway = create_external_runtime_gateway(
        live_intelligence_agent=(
            create_live_intelligence_agent()
        ),
        podcast_discovery_agent=(
            create_podcast_discovery_agent()
        ),
        podcast_providers=(
            create_podcast_providers()
        ),
        governance=(
            create_governance()
        ),
    )

    runtime_service = (
        factory.create_external_runtime_service(
            gateway=gateway,
        )
    )

    configure_external_runtime_service(
        runtime_service,
    )

    _bootstrapped = True


def get_external_runtime_service() -> ExternalRuntimeService:
    """
    Backward-compatible Runtime accessor.
    """

    return _get_external_runtime_service()


def reset_bootstrap() -> None:
    """
    Test-only reset.
    """

    global _bootstrapped

    _bootstrapped = False

class BootstrapLiveIntelligenceSearchProvider(
    SearchProvider,
):
    """
    Deterministic hackathon search provider.

    Implements the provider-independent contract.

    Production replacement:
        real search provider adapter.

    This class does not belong to Runtime.
    It exists only as bootstrap wiring.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:

        results = [
            SearchResult(
                title=(
                    "AI Regulation Update"
                ),
                url=(
                    "https://example.com/"
                    "ai-regulation"
                ),
                content=(
                    "Latest developments in "
                    "artificial intelligence "
                    "regulation and technology."
                ),
                source="hackathon-demo",
            ),
            SearchResult(
                title=(
                    "Artificial Intelligence "
                    "Technology News"
                ),
                url=(
                    "https://example.com/"
                    "ai-news"
                ),
                content=(
                    "Recent artificial "
                    "intelligence technology "
                    "updates."
                ),
                source="hackathon-demo",
            ),
        ]

        return results[:max_results]


class BootstrapIntelligenceProcessor(
    IntelligenceProcessor,
):
    """
    Deterministic intelligence processor.

    Temporary hackathon implementation.

    Future replacement:
        approved LLM-based processor.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:

        summary = (
            f"AI intelligence summary "
            f"adapted for {learner_level} "
            f"learner in {language}."
        )

        key_information = [
            "Artificial intelligence regulation",
            "Technology developments",
        ]

        return (
            summary,
            key_information,
        )

    Create Live Intelligence Agent.

        ),
    )

"""
EasyChatBot API Application.

Hosts the user interaction layer.

The API application is responsible only for:

- creating the FastAPI application;
- registering API routes;
- exposing the application health endpoint;
- triggering application composition bootstrap during startup;
- allowing the local frontend to communicate with the API.

Business logic belongs to application services.

Agent execution belongs to the agent/runtime layer.

Provider construction belongs to the composition layer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from backend.api.routes import router
from backend.api.character_theme_routes import (
    router as character_theme_router,
)
from backend.api.character_routes import (
    router as character_router,
)
from backend.application.bootstrap import bootstrap_application

from app.interfaces.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_router import (
    router as operations_center_dashboard_router,
)

from backend.app.api.cybersecurity_intelligence import (
    router as cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
)


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(
    application: FastAPI,
):
    """
    Manage application startup and shutdown lifecycle.

    Startup:
        bootstrap application dependencies.

    Shutdown:
        reserved for future controlled cleanup.

    This layer does not:

    - execute agents;
    - execute capabilities;
    - create providers;
    - create Runtime components.
    """

    bootstrap_application()

    yield


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    """
    Create the EasyChatBot FastAPI application.

    Application composition is intentionally separated
    from the HTTP layer.

    This function:

    - creates the FastAPI application;
    - configures local frontend access;
    - registers API routes;
    - attaches lifecycle management.

    It does not:

    - execute agents;
    - execute capabilities;
    - call providers directly;
    - create Runtime executors.
    """

    application = FastAPI(
        title="EasyChatBot",
        description="AI companion learning platform",
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )

    # =========================================================
    # Local Frontend CORS
    # =========================================================

    application.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=[
            "http://127.0.0.1:5500",
            "http://localhost:5500",
            "http://127.0.0.1:5501",
            "http://localhost:5501",
            "http://localhost:3000",
            "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
        ],
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    # =========================================================
    # Existing API Routes
    # =========================================================

    application.include_router(
        router,
    )

    # =========================================================
    # Character Theme Routes
    # =========================================================

    application.include_router(
        character_theme_router,
    )

    # =========================================================
    # Character Catalogue Routes
    # =========================================================

    application.include_router(
        character_router,
    )

    # =========================================================
    # AI Operations Center Routes
    # =========================================================
    #
    # Read-only Operations Center boundary.
    #
    # Exposes:
    #
    #     GET /api/operations-center/dashboard
    #     GET /api/operations-center/operational-events
    #
    # The router delegates to the already-composed application
    # services. The FastAPI bootstrap does not create providers,
    # Runtime components, repositories, or operational events.

    application.include_router(
        operations_center_dashboard_router,
    )

    # =========================================================
    # Cybersecurity Intelligence Routes
    # =========================================================
    #
    # Read-only Cybersecurity Intelligence boundary.
    #
    # Exposes:
    #
    #     GET /api/cybersecurity-center/intelligence
    #
    # The router delegates to the existing
    # CybersecurityIntelligenceService and Runtime/Agent
    # execution path.
    #
    # This API does not:
    #
    # - execute remediation;
    # - modify infrastructure;
    # - call external security providers;
    # - bypass Runtime controls.

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
    )

    return application


app = create_app()


@app.get("/")
def health() -> dict[str, str]:
    """
    Return application health status.
    """

    return {
        "status": "EasyChatBot running",
        "architecture": (
            "Runtime + Agents + Memory"
        ),
    }

    create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime()

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(
    application: FastAPI,
):
    """
    Application lifecycle.

    Starts:
    - cybersecurity scheduler runtime.

    Stops:
    - cybersecurity scheduler runtime.

    No business logic belongs here.
    """


    try:

        await scheduler_runtime.start()


    except Exception as exc:

        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler startup failed:",
            exc,
        )



    yield



    try:

        await scheduler_runtime.stop()


    except Exception as exc:

        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler shutdown failed:",
            exc,
        )



def create_application() -> FastAPI:
    """
    Create and configure FastAPI application.
    """


    application = FastAPI(

        title="EasyChatBot API",

        description=(
            "AI character learning platform API"
        ),

        version="0.1.0",

        lifespan=lifespan,

    )



    #
    # CORS
    #

    application.add_middleware(

        CORSMiddleware,

        allow_origins=[

            "http://localhost:3000",

        ],

        allow_credentials=True,

        allow_methods=[

            "*",

        ],

        allow_headers=[

            "*",

        ],

    )



    #
    # Voice APIs
    #

    application.include_router(
        voice_calls_router,
    )


    application.include_router(
        voice_reconnect_router,
    )


    application.include_router(
        voice_call_trigger_router,
    )


    application.include_router(
        voice_notification_acceptance_router,
    )



    #
    # AI Operations Center
    #

    application.include_router(
        operations_center_router,
    )



    #
    # Governance Audit API
    #
    # Provides immutable evidence visibility.
    #

    application.include_router(
        governance_audit_router,
    )



    #
    # Operations Center Governance API
    #
    # Provides governance dashboard state.
    #

    application.include_router(
        governance_operations_router,
    )



    #
    # Governance Evidence Timeline API
    #
    # Provides evidence explorer data.
    #

    application.include_router(
        governance_evidence_router,
    )



    #
    # Governance Change Lifecycle API
    #
    # Approval -> Change -> Audit lifecycle.
    #

    application.include_router(
        governance_change_lifecycle_router,
    )



    #
    # Cybersecurity Intelligence
    #

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
    )



    #
    # Cybersecurity Decision Support
    #

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_decision_support_router,
    )



    #
    # Cybersecurity Reports
    #

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_reports_router,
    )



    #
    # Cybersecurity Live Reports
    #

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_live_reports_router,
    )


    return application



app = create_application()

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Callable, Awaitable

from app.application.beta_access.beta_access_service import BetaAccessService

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class BetaTelegramStartHandler:
    """Adapter for /start <token>. Only private chats may bind Beta users."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        beta_access_service: BetaAccessService,
        reply: Callable[[str, object], Awaitable[None]],
    ) -> None:
        self._access = beta_access_service
        self._reply = reply

    async def handle(
        self,
        *,
        token: str | None,
        telegram_user_id: int,
        chat_id: int,
        chat_type: str,
    ) -> None:
        if chat_type != "private":
            await self._reply(
                "Please open a private chat with the Academy bot to connect your classroom.",
                None,
            )
            return

        if not token:
            await self._reply(
                "Your classroom connection link is missing or invalid.",
                None,
            )
            return

        try:
            user = await self._access.validate_and_bind(
                token=token,
                telegram_id=telegram_user_id,
                chat_id=chat_id,
            )
        except (ValueError, PermissionError) as exc:
            logger.warning(
                "Telegram Beta binding rejected telegram_id=%s reason=%s",
                telegram_user_id,
                exc,
            )
            await self._reply(str(exc), None)
            return

        await self._reply(
            (
                "Welcome to EasyChatBot Academy. "
                f"Your classroom is connected to World '{user.active_world_id}'."
            ),
            user,
        )

from __future__ import annotations


class BetaTelegramAdapter:
    """
    Thin adapter for python-telegram-bot.

    The adapter deliberately keeps Telegram transport concerns outside
    BetaAccessService.
    """

    def __init__(self, *, start_handler, private_chat_gate):
        self._start_handler = start_handler
        self._private_chat_gate = private_chat_gate

    async def handle_start(self, update, context):
        telegram_user_id = str(update.effective_user.id)
        chat_id = str(update.effective_chat.id)
        args = context.args or []

        token = args[0] if args else ""

        result = self._start_handler.handle(
            token=token,
            telegram_id=telegram_user_id,
            chat_id=chat_id,
        )

        await update.effective_message.reply_text(result.message)

    def is_private_chat_allowed(self, *, user_id: str, chat_type: str) -> bool:
        return self._private_chat_gate.authorize(
            user_id=user_id,
            chat_type=chat_type,
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.beta_access.beta_access_service import BetaAccessService


class BetaPrivateChatGate:
    """Authorization gate for all Beta classroom interactions."""

    def __init__(self, *, beta_access_service: BetaAccessService) -> None:
        self._access = beta_access_service

    async def authorize(self, *, telegram_user_id: int) -> bool:
        try:
            await self._access.authorize_private_chat(telegram_user_id)
        except (ValueError, PermissionError):
            return False
        return True

    async def require(self, *, telegram_user_id: int):
        return await self._access.authorize_private_chat(telegram_user_id)

from __future__ import annotations


class BetaTelegramPrivateChatGate:
    """
    Transport-level gate.

    Beta classroom access is permitted only from a Telegram private chat.
    """

    def __init__(self, *, beta_access_service):
        self._beta_access_service = beta_access_service

    def authorize(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        chat_type: str,
    ) -> bool:
        if chat_type != "private":
            return False

        return self._beta_access_service.is_active(user_id=user_id)

FILE: .\backend\app\application\beta_access\beta_access_service.py

============================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import logging
import secrets
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Iterator

from .models import BetaConnectionToken, BetaUserState, TelegramBinding
from .repositories import (
    BetaTokenRepository,
    BetaUserRepository,
    TelegramBindingRepository,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BetaConnectionTokenResult:
    """
    Result of creating a Beta Telegram connection token.

    Compatibility:
    - ``result.token`` provides the raw token.
    - ``result.connection`` provides the persisted token record.
    - Tuple unpacking remains supported::

          raw_token, connection = result

    The raw token is never persisted. Only its SHA-256 hash is stored.
    """

    token: str
    connection: BetaConnectionToken

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[object]:
        yield self.token
        yield self.connection


class BetaAccessService:
    """Application boundary for Beta eligibility and Telegram binding."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        user_repository: BetaUserRepository,
        token_repository: BetaTokenRepository,
        binding_repository: TelegramBindingRepository,
        expiration_minutes: int = 15,
    ) -> None:
        if expiration_minutes <= 0:
            raise ValueError("expiration_minutes must be positive.")

        self._users = user_repository
        self._tokens = token_repository
        self._bindings = binding_repository
        self._expiration_minutes = expiration_minutes

    async def approve_beta(self, user_id: str) -> BetaUserState:
        user = await self._require_user(user_id)

        if not user.email_verified:
            raise ValueError(
                "Email must be verified before Beta approval."
            )

        return await self._users.save(
            replace(user, beta_approved=True)
        )

    async def assign_world(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        world_id: str,
    ) -> BetaUserState:
        if not world_id:
            raise ValueError("world_id is required.")

        user = await self._require_user(user_id)

        if not user.email_verified:
            raise ValueError(
                "Email must be verified before World assignment."
            )

        if not user.beta_approved:
            raise ValueError("User is not Beta approved.")

        return await self._users.save(
            replace(
                user,
                active_world_id=world_id,
            )
        )

    async def create_connection_token(
        self,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> BetaConnectionTokenResult:
        """
        Create a short-lived one-time Telegram connection token.

        The raw token is returned to the application layer but is never
        persisted. Only its SHA-256 hash is stored.
        """
        user = await self._require_user(user_id)

        if not user.email_verified:
            raise ValueError("Email is not verified.")

        if not user.beta_approved:
            raise ValueError("User is not Beta approved.")

        if not user.active_world_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "User must have a Learning World before Telegram access."
            )

        raw_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)

        token = BetaConnectionToken(
            token_id=secrets.token_hex(16),
            user_id=user.user_id,
            token_hash=self.hash_token(raw_token),
            expires_at=(
                datetime.now(timezone.utc)
                + timedelta(minutes=self._expiration_minutes)
            ),
        )

        await self._tokens.save(token)

        logger.info(
            "Beta Telegram token created user_id=%s",
            user.user_id,
        )

        return BetaConnectionTokenResult(
            token=raw_token,
            connection=token,
        )

    async def validate_and_bind(
        self,
        *,
        token: str,
        telegram_id: int,
        chat_id: int,
    ) -> BetaUserState:
        if not token:
            raise ValueError(
                "Connection token is required."
            )

        if telegram_id <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Telegram ID must be positive."
            )

        if chat_id == 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Chat ID is required."
            )

        record = await self._tokens.get_by_hash(
            self.hash_token(token)
        )

        if record is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Invalid connection token."
            )

        if record.used:
            raise ValueError(
                "Connection token already used."
            )

        expires_at = record.expires_at

        if expires_at.tzinfo is None:
            expires_at = expires_at.replace(
                tzinfo=timezone.utc
            )

        if expires_at <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
            raise ValueError(
                "Connection token expired."
            )

        user = await self._require_user(
            record.user_id
        )

        if not user.eligible:
            raise ValueError(
                "User is not eligible for Beta Telegram access."
            )

        if not user.active_world_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "User has no assigned Learning World."
            )

        existing = await self._users.get_by_telegram_id(
            telegram_id
        )

        if (
            existing is not None
            and existing.user_id != user.user_id
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Telegram account is already connected "
                "to another user."
            )

        existing_binding = await self._bindings.get_by_user_id(
            user.user_id
        )

        if (
            existing_binding is not None
            and existing_binding.telegram_user_id
            != str(telegram_id)
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "User already has another Telegram binding."
            )

        await self._bindings.save(
            TelegramBinding(
                binding_id=secrets.token_hex(16),
                user_id=user.user_id,
                telegram_user_id=str(telegram_id),
                chat_id=str(chat_id),
                connected_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )
        )

        await self._tokens.mark_used(
            replace(
                record,
                used_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )
        )

        return await self._users.save(
            replace(
                user,
                telegram_id=telegram_id,
            )
        )

    async def authorize_private_chat(
        self,
        telegram_id: int,
    ) -> BetaUserState:
        if telegram_id <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Telegram ID must be positive."
            )

        user = await self._users.get_by_telegram_id(
            telegram_id
        )

        if user is None:
            raise PermissionError(
                "Telegram account is not connected."
            )

        if not user.active:
            raise PermissionError(
                "User is connected but is not ACTIVE "
                "for a Learning World."
            )

        return user

    @staticmethod
    def hash_token(token: str) -> str:
        return hashlib.sha256(
            token.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()

    async def _require_user(
        self,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> BetaUserState:
        user = await self._users.get(user_id)

        if user is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Academy user does not exist."
            )

        return user

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum


class BetaAccessStatus(StrEnum):
    PENDING_VERIFICATION = "PENDING_VERIFICATION"
    PENDING_APPROVAL = "PENDING_APPROVAL"
    ELIGIBLE = "ELIGIBLE"
    TELEGRAM_CONNECTED = "TELEGRAM_CONNECTED"
    ACTIVE = "ACTIVE"
    DENIED = "DENIED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BetaUserState:
    user_id: str
    email: str
    email_verified: bool
    beta_approved: bool
    telegram_id: int | None = None
    active_world_id: str | None = None

    @property
    def eligible(self) -> bool:
        return self.email_verified and self.beta_approved

    @property
    def active(self) -> bool:
        return (
            self.eligible
            and self.telegram_id is not None
            and self.active_world_id is not None
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BetaConnectionToken:
    token_id: str
    user_id: str
    token_hash: str
    expires_at: datetime
    used_at: datetime | None = None

    @property
    def used(self) -> bool:
        return self.used_at is not None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TelegramBinding:
    binding_id: str
    user_id: str
    telegram_user_id: str
    chat_id: str
    connected_at: datetime

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol

from .models import BetaConnectionToken, BetaUserState, TelegramBinding


class BetaUserRepository(Protocol):
    async def get(self, user_id: str) -> BetaUserState | None: ...
    async def get_by_telegram_id(self, telegram_id: int) -> BetaUserState | None: ...
    async def save(self, user: BetaUserState) -> BetaUserState: ...


class BetaTokenRepository(Protocol):
    async def get_by_hash(self, token_hash: str) -> BetaConnectionToken | None: ...
    async def save(self, token: BetaConnectionToken) -> BetaConnectionToken: ...
    async def mark_used(self, token: BetaConnectionToken) -> BetaConnectionToken: ...


class TelegramBindingRepository(Protocol):
    async def get_by_telegram_user_id(self, telegram_user_id: str) -> TelegramBinding | None: ...
    async def get_by_user_id(self, user_id: str) -> TelegramBinding | None: ...
    async def save(self, binding: TelegramBinding) -> TelegramBinding: ...

FILE: .\backend\app\infrastructure\persistence\database.py

from __future__ import annotations

import os

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, sessionmaker, Session


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


def database_url() -> str:
    value = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")
    if not value:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "DATABASE_URL is required for Beta Telegram persistence."
        )
    return value


def create_database_engine():
    return create_engine(
        database_url(),
        pool_pre_ping=True,
        future=True,
    )


def create_session_factory(engine):
    return sessionmaker(
        bind=engine,
        class_=Session,
        expire_on_commit=False,
    )

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, ForeignKey, String, UniqueConstraint
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from .database import Base


class BetaUserRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "beta_users"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), primary_key=True)
    email: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(320), nullable=False, unique=True, index=True)
    email_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    approved: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    active_world_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True, index=True)
    telegram_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True, unique=True, index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)


class BetaConnectionTokenRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "beta_connection_tokens"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), primary_key=True)
    token_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False, unique=True, index=True)
    user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("beta_users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)
    expires_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    used_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)


class TelegramBindingRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "telegram_bindings"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("telegram_user_id", name="uq_telegram_binding_telegram_user"),
        UniqueConstraint("user_id", name="uq_telegram_binding_beta_user"),
    )

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), primary_key=True)
    user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("beta_users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)
    telegram_user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    chat_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    connected_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Optional

from app.application.beta_access.models import (
    BetaConnectionToken,
    BetaUserState,
    TelegramBinding,
)


class InMemoryBetaUserRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._users: dict[str, BetaUserState] = {}

    async def save(self, user: BetaUserState) -> BetaUserState:
        self._users[user.user_id] = user
        return user

    async def get(
        self,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> Optional[BetaUserState]:
        return self._users.get(user_id)

    async def get_by_telegram_id(
        self,
        telegram_id: int,
    ) -> Optional[BetaUserState]:
        for user in self._users.values():
            if getattr(user, "telegram_id", None) == telegram_id:
                return user

        return None


class InMemoryBetaTokenRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._tokens: dict[str, BetaConnectionToken] = {}

    async def save(
        self,
        token: BetaConnectionToken,
    ) -> BetaConnectionToken:
        self._tokens[token.token_id] = token
        return token

    async def get(
        self,
        token_id: str,
    ) -> Optional[BetaConnectionToken]:
        return self._tokens.get(token_id)

    async def get_by_hash(
        self,
        token_hash: str,
    ) -> Optional[BetaConnectionToken]:
        for token in self._tokens.values():
            if token.token_hash == token_hash:
                return token

        return None

    async def mark_used(
        self,
        token: BetaConnectionToken,
    ) -> BetaConnectionToken:
        """
        Persist the token with its used_at timestamp.

        The application service passes an immutable replacement of
        the existing token, so the in-memory repository simply replaces
        the stored record.
        """
        self._tokens[token.token_id] = token
        return token


class InMemoryTelegramBindingRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._bindings: dict[str, TelegramBinding] = {}

    async def save(
        self,
        binding: TelegramBinding,
    ) -> TelegramBinding:
        self._bindings[binding.telegram_user_id] = binding
        return binding

    async def get_by_telegram_user_id(
        self,
        telegram_user_id: str,
    ) -> Optional[TelegramBinding]:
        return self._bindings.get(telegram_user_id)

    async def get_by_user_id(
        self,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> Optional[TelegramBinding]:
        for binding in self._bindings.values():
            if binding.user_id == user_id:
                return binding

        return None

"""
Conversation Runtime.

Coordinates AI responses.

Does NOT:
- Call vendors directly.
- Manage LiveKit.
- Store conversations.
"""


from __future__ import annotations


from dataclasses import dataclass


from app.providers.llm.llm_provider import (
    LLMProvider,
)



@dataclass(
    frozen=True,
    slots=True,
)
class ConversationRuntime:


    llm_provider: LLMProvider



    async def respond(
        self,
        *,
        character_instruction: str,
        learner_message: str,
    ) -> str:


        return await self.llm_provider.generate(
            system_prompt=character_instruction,
            user_message=learner_message,
        )

"""
Voice AI dependency composition.

Builds AI Runtime dependencies required
by realtime voice execution.

Responsibilities:
- Compose conversation runtime.
- Compose speech runtime.
- Keep provider selection outside infrastructure.
- Provide application-ready dependencies.

Does NOT:
- Execute calls.
- Handle HTTP.
- Manage LiveKit rooms.
- Store secrets.
- Call providers directly.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from app.ai_runtime.conversation.conversation_runtime import (
    ConversationRuntime,
)


from app.ai_runtime.voice.voice_speech_runtime import (
    VoiceSpeechRuntime,
)


from app.providers.llm.llm_provider_factory import (
    LLMProviderFactory,
)


from app.providers.speech.speech_provider_factory import (
    SpeechProviderFactory,
)


from app.configuration.providers.speech_provider_config import (
    SpeechProviderConfig,
)



def build_conversation_runtime() -> ConversationRuntime:
    """
    Build EasyChatBot AI conversation runtime.
    """

    provider = (
        LLMProviderFactory.create()
    )


    return ConversationRuntime(
        llm_provider=provider,
    )



def build_voice_speech_runtime() -> VoiceSpeechRuntime:
    """
    Build EasyChatBot voice speech runtime.
    """

    config = SpeechProviderConfig(
        provider_name="openai",
        model_name="gpt-4o-mini-tts",
        default_language="fr",
    )


    provider = (
        SpeechProviderFactory.create(
            config,
        )
    )


    return VoiceSpeechRuntime(
        speech_provider=provider,
    )



__all__ = [
    "build_conversation_runtime",
    "build_voice_speech_runtime",
]

"""
LLM Provider Contract.

Defines abstraction for language model providers.

Supported providers:
- OpenAI
- Ollama
- Future enterprise providers

Does NOT:
- Manage conversations.
- Manage users.
- Manage memory.
- Manage LiveKit.
"""


from __future__ import annotations


from abc import ABC, abstractmethod


class LLMProvider(
    ABC
):
    """
    Abstract LLM provider.
    """


    @abstractmethod
    async def generate(
        self,
        *,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_message: str,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Generate model response.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError

import pytest

from app.application.beta_access.beta_access_service import BetaAccessService
from app.application.beta_access.models import BetaUserState
from app.infrastructure.persistence.in_memory_beta_access_repository import (
    InMemoryBetaTokenRepository,
    InMemoryBetaUserRepository,
    InMemoryTelegramBindingRepository,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_telegram_identity_cannot_be_owned_by_two_users():
    users = InMemoryBetaUserRepository()
    tokens = InMemoryBetaTokenRepository()
    bindings = InMemoryTelegramBindingRepository()

    service = BetaAccessService(
        user_repository=users,
        token_repository=tokens,
        binding_repository=bindings,
    )

    for user_id in ("a", "b"):
        await users.save(
            BetaUserState(
                user_id=user_id,
                email=f"{user_id}@example.com",
                email_verified=True,
                beta_approved=True,
                active_world_id="world-fr",
            )
        )

    first = await service.create_connection_token("a")
    await service.validate_and_bind(
        token=first.token,
        telegram_id=111,
        chat_id=111,
    )

    second = await service.create_connection_token("b")

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="already connected"):
        await service.validate_and_bind(
            token=second.token,
            telegram_id=111,
            chat_id=111,
        )

def test_generated_token_is_hashed():

    assert raw
    assert hashed
    assert raw != hashed
    assert BetaConnectionTokenService.hash_token(raw) == hashed

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.beta_access.beta_access_service import BetaAccessService
from app.application.beta_access.models import BetaUserState
from app.application.beta_access.email_workflow import BetaTelegramInvitationService
from app.infrastructure.email.in_memory_beta_email_sender import InMemoryBetaEmailSender
from app.interfaces.telegram.beta_start_handler import BetaTelegramStartHandler
from app.interfaces.telegram.private_chat_gate import BetaPrivateChatGate


class Users:
    def __init__(self):
        self.items = {}
    async def get(self, user_id):
        return self.items.get(user_id)
    async def get_by_telegram_id(self, telegram_id):
        return next((u for u in self.items.values() if u.telegram_id == telegram_id), None)
    async def save(self, user):
        self.items[user.user_id] = user
        return user


class Tokens:
    def __init__(self):
        self.items = {}
    async def get_by_hash(self, token_hash):
        return next((t for t in self.items.values() if t.token_hash == token_hash), None)
    async def save(self, token):
        self.items[token.token_id] = token
        return token
    async def mark_used(self, token):
        self.items[token.token_id] = token
        return token


class Bindings:
    def __init__(self):
        self.items = {}
    async def get_by_telegram_user_id(self, telegram_user_id):
        return next((b for b in self.items.values() if b.telegram_user_id == telegram_user_id), None)
    async def get_by_user_id(self, user_id):
        return next((b for b in self.items.values() if b.user_id == user_id), None)
    async def save(self, binding):
        self.items[binding.binding_id] = binding
        return binding


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_complete_beta_telegram_vertical_slice():
    users = Users()
    tokens = Tokens()
    bindings = Bindings()

    users.items["u1"] = BetaUserState(
        user_id="u1",
        email="learner@example.com",
        email_verified=True,
        beta_approved=True,
        active_world_id="fr_world",
    )

    access = BetaAccessService(
        user_repository=users,
        token_repository=tokens,
        binding_repository=bindings,
    )

    email = InMemoryBetaEmailSender()
    invitation = BetaTelegramInvitationService(
        beta_access_service=access,
        email_sender=email,
        telegram_bot_username="EasyChatBotAcademyBot",
    )

    deep_link = await invitation.send_invitation(user_id="u1")
    raw_token = deep_link.split("?start=", 1)[1]

    replies = []

    async def reply(message, user):
        replies.append(message)

    handler = BetaTelegramStartHandler(
        beta_access_service=access,
        reply=reply,
    )

    await handler.handle(
        token=raw_token,
        telegram_user_id=12345,
        chat_id=12345,
        chat_type="private",
    )

    assert email.messages[0]["email"] == "learner@example.com"
    assert "start=" in email.messages[0]["classroom_url"]
    assert replies
    assert "connected" in replies[0].lower()

    gate = BetaPrivateChatGate(beta_access_service=access)
    assert await gate.authorize(telegram_user_id=12345) is True


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_token_is_single_use():
    users = Users()
    tokens = Tokens()
    bindings = Bindings()

    users.items["u1"] = BetaUserState(
        user_id="u1",
        email="learner@example.com",
        email_verified=True,
        beta_approved=True,
        active_world_id="fr_world",
    )

    access = BetaAccessService(
        user_repository=users,
        token_repository=tokens,
        binding_repository=bindings,
    )

    raw_token, _ = await access.create_connection_token("u1")

    await access.validate_and_bind(
        token=raw_token,
        telegram_id=100,
        chat_id=100,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="already used"):
        await access.validate_and_bind(
            token=raw_token,
            telegram_id=101,
            chat_id=101,
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_group_chat_cannot_bind():
    users = Users()
    tokens = Tokens()
    bindings = Bindings()

    users.items["u1"] = BetaUserState(
        user_id="u1",
        email="learner@example.com",
        email_verified=True,
        beta_approved=True,
        active_world_id="fr_world",
    )

    access = BetaAccessService(
        user_repository=users,
        token_repository=tokens,
        binding_repository=bindings,
    )

    raw_token, _ = await access.create_connection_token("u1")

    replies = []

    async def reply(message, user):
        replies.append(message)

    handler = BetaTelegramStartHandler(
        beta_access_service=access,
        reply=reply,
    )

    await handler.handle(
        token=raw_token,
        telegram_user_id=1,
        chat_id=-100,
        chat_type="group",
    )

    assert "private" in replies[0].lower()

from app.interfaces.telegram.beta_telegram_private_chat_gate import (

class StubAccess:

    def is_active(self, *, user_id: str) -> bool:

def test_private_chat_gate_rejects_group():

        beta_access_service=StubAccess(True)
    )

    )

        beta_access_service=StubAccess(False)
    )
    assert gate.authorize(user_id="u1", chat_type="private") is False

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
from logging.config import fileConfig

from alembic import context
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config, pool

# Load the repository-level .env before accessing DATABASE_URL.
# Repository root:
# C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\.env
load_dotenv(
    os.path.abspath(
        os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", ".env")
    )
)

# Make backend/ importable so Alembic can load the application modules.
sys.path.insert(
    0,
    os.path.abspath(
        os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..")
    ),
)

from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base, database_url
from app.infrastructure.persistence import beta_access_models  # noqa: F401


config = context.config

if config.config_file_name is not None:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

target_metadata = Base.metadata


def get_database_url() -> str:
    """Return the configured database URL."""
    return database_url()


def run_migrations_offline() -> None:
    """Run migrations without creating a database connection."""
    url = get_database_url()

    context.configure(
        url=url,
        target_metadata=target_metadata,
        literal_binds=True,
        dialect_opts={"paramstyle": "named"},
        compare_type=True,
    )

    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()


def run_migrations_online() -> None:
    """Run migrations using a live database connection."""
    configuration = config.get_section(config.config_ini_section) or {}
    configuration["sqlalchemy.url"] = get_database_url()

    connectable = engine_from_config(
        configuration,
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )

    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(
            connection=connection,
            target_metadata=target_metadata,
            compare_type=True,
        )

        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()


if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()
